2026-10-24T06:52:12.8435872Z   QWEN_COMMIT_MESSAGE: [cluster:firmus] [nodes:2] [profile] Serve the 8793.13 configuration without FC1 early release and with exact hardware FP8 Decode QK-norm/RoPE

Baseline: komoriyuta's run 40059609107 (bf59ba6), 8793.13 (raw 9009.7), cap 160, concurrency 240. Two changes against it:

1. FC1 early release leaves the serving DeepGEMM overlays. Kernel bench 40069880580 (3e2e0c1, image 40064516380) measured it 1.1-2.0% slower on every Prefill case, for example real43-replica4 1.424 -> 1.452 ms, with identical output hashes. The Genkai one-GPU probe had shown -2.5 to -4% for it, and the 8793.13 profile had Prefill MegaMoE at 1561 us per layer against 1526 us before it.
2. patches/sglang/decode-qknorm-fast-fp8.patch replaces the CUDA __NV_NOSAT software FP8 conversion (through double, still present in the CUDA 13.0 header) in qknorm_rope_decode_kernel with the hardware SATFINITE conversion plus the exact overflow/NaN repair already used in komoriyuta's Prefill fast kernel. Genkai probe 40066392486 matched old/new output bitwise for rows 0-193, int32/int64 positions, cache slot zero, changed-input graphs and all 65,536 BF16 patterns. Timings there were 8.27 -> 5.00 us at 128 rows and 9.53 -> 5.34 us at 160 rows. The serving gate runs the exhaustive conversion check.

The same kernel bench also measured the Prefill independent-WG candidates A and B at 18-36% and 25-44% slower. They are not enabled: DG_SM90_PREFILL_INDEPENDENT_WG stays unset, which keeps the existing instantiation in image 40064516380.

Image 40064516380 (build of 65b3741): komoriyuta's image content plus the Prefill independent-WG host opt-in, sha256 112ff6f0831d21e703b686df96bf950501d51a75c67950ea6a707b80197638bf. The serving scripts are identical to komoriyuta's branch, and the in-job FA3/dense selection is unchanged.

Expected effect, estimated: Prefill MegaMoE about -35 us per layer (Prefill about -1.3%) and Decode about -0.4 ms per step (about -2%).
